### [1] [Beyond the Autoregressive Horizon: A Comprehensive Survey of Diffusion Models, World Modelling, and State Space Models for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23690)
*Kishan Maharaj,Ashita Saxena,Srikanth Tamilselvam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判性分析了自回归语言模型在代码智能中的结构性局限，探讨了扩散模型、代码世界模型、状态空间模型等新兴范式，并融合认知神经科学视角提出"系统2"代码生成智能体的发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能研究过度集中于自回归（AR）语言模型，但AR模型的下一个token预测范式在全局规划、长程依赖维护和执行语义 grounding 方面存在结构性瓶颈，亟需探索能突破这些限制的新架构。

Method: 通过分析三类非AR方法：扩散模型（整体去噪生成以捕捉长程句法约束）、代码世界模型（模拟执行状态支持推理）、状态空间模型（线性时间复杂度处理超长上下文），并结合认知神经科学中"系统2"思维的概念，提出未来研究方向。

Result: 指出扩散模型能捕捉AR模型遗漏的长程句法约束；代码世界模型通过执行状态模拟增强推理能力；状态空间模型实现线性效率的上下文处理。这些方法可能突破AR模型在逻辑和扩展性方面的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 未来代码智能发展需超越AR范式的下个token预测，融合多种非自回归架构并结合认知科学中系统2思维（慢思考、规划、推理），构建新一代代码生成智能体。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) language models have driven significant progress in automated software engineering, enabling powerful code generation and assistance systems. However, the next-token prediction paradigm introduces structural limitations for code reasoning, including restricted global planning, challenges in maintaining long-range dependencies, and limited grounding in program execution semantics. Noting the heavy skewness of existing literature towards AR models, we discuss emerging paradigms that could potentially overcome the logic and scaling bottlenecks of next-token prediction by unlocking next-generation architectural capabilities for code intelligence. Specifically, we discuss the potential of Diffusion Models, which generate code via holistic denoising that captures long-range syntactic constraints often missed by AR models. We also discuss Code World Models (CWMs), which simulate execution states to support reasoning, and State Space Models (SSMs), which provide linear-time efficiency for massive contexts. By connecting these developments with findings from cognitive neuroscience, we outline directions for developing "System 2" code generation agents.

</details>


### [2] [From Heuristics to Transformers: A Comprehensive Survey of Type Inference from Stripped Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23692)
*Hua Zheng,Yuhang Guo,Kuanishbay Sadatdiynov,Cheng Wen,Muhammad Sadiq,Dugang Liu,Jawwad Ahmed Shamsi,Anam Qureshi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了从剥离二进制文件中恢复高级类型信息的技术演进路线：从早期基于规则和约束求解的方法（如BITY、BinSub），到上下文感知的神经网络模型（如EKLAVYA、CATI），再到当前最先进的Transformer和图神经网络架构（如SeeType、TYGR）。同时指出了优化导致语义丢失和结构类型恢复等核心挑战，并提出了神经符号推理的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从剥离二进制文件中恢复高级类型信息是逆向工程、漏洞分析和反编译的基础性关键任务，但符号表和调试信息的缺失使得该问题极具挑战性。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，按技术演进阶段梳理二进制类型推断方法：早期基于规则的启发式方法→约束求解（BITY、BinSub）→上下文感知神经模型（EKLAVYA、CATI）→Transformer与GNN架构（SeeType、TYGR），分析各阶段的原理与特点。

Result: 清晰呈现了二进制类型推断技术从符号化到数据驱动的完整演进路径，识别出因果优化导致的语义丢失和结构类型恢复两大核心难题。

Conclusion: 二进制类型推断正在向神经符号推理方向演进，该方向有望融合数据驱动方法的灵活性与符号方法的可解释性，突破当前面临的核心挑战。

Abstract: The recovery of high-level type information from stripped binaries-executables devoid of symbol tables and debugging information-is a cornerstone of software reverse engineering, vulnerability analysis, and decompilation. This survey tracks the evolution of binary type inference from early rule-based heuristics and static analysis to modern deep learning architectures. We analyze the shift from "duck typing" and constraint-solving techniques (e.g., BITY, BinSub) to context-aware neural models (e.g., EKLAVYA, CATI) and finally to state-of-the-art Transformer and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures (e.g., SeeType, TYGR). We identify core challenges, including optimization-induced semantics loss and structural type recovery, and propose future research directions in neuro-symbolic inference.

</details>


### [3] [Privacy Engineering: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23696)
*Nemania Borovits,Damian Andrew Tamburri,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过系统性文献综述（90篇研究，2018-2025），识别出隐私工程的十三个维度，形成两大核心集群（PETs/PM/VT 和 GA/TC/OM），其中MS作为中介；生命周期映射显示各维度在不同阶段的分布；领域偏好改变权重但不改变结构；IRM、LM和DMPL是未来工作重点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR下的隐私义务日益影响软件工程，需要对隐私工程实践进行系统性的梳理和结构化，以提供可操作的指导框架和研究路线图。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，结合主题综合（thematic synthesis），筛选2018至2025年间90篇相关研究，归纳隐私工程的核心维度并分析其生命周期分布和领域差异性。

Result: 识别出13个隐私工程维度，形成两个核心集群（PETs/PM/VT 与 GA/TC/OM），MS作为中介；生命周期中MS和GA集中在需求与设计阶段，PETs/PM/VT/TC集中在实现与验证阶段，GA/OM/DSRM/IRM/LM集中在运维与退役阶段；领域（医疗、IoT、Web测量、AI/ML）仅改变维度权重而不改变整体结构；IRM、LM和DMPL作为较少关注的方向被指出。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可在实践中使用的隐私工程全景图，以及一个可复用的评估与更新框架，同时指出了隐私工程中值得未来深入研究的薄弱方向。

Abstract: Privacy obligations under GDPR increasingly shape software engineering. We synthesize 90 studies from 2018 to 2025 using a systematic review with thematic synthesis to chart privacy engineering. Thirteen dimensions form two recurrent cores: Privacy Enhancing Technologies (PETs) with Privacy Metrics (PM) and Verification and Testing (VT) and Governance and Accountability (GA) with Transparency and Communication (TC) and Organizational Measures (OM). Modeling and Specification (MS) mediates between the cores. Lifecycle mapping shows concentrations at requirements and design (MS, GA), at implementation and verification (PETs, VT, PM, TC) and at operation and decommissioning (GA, OM, Data Subject Rights Management (DSRM), Incident Response and Management (IRM), Lifelong Management (LM)). Handoffs link models to rules and tests, mechanisms to metrics and deployments such as enclaves and ledgers to governance records. Domains reweight but do not alter structure: healthcare weights GA with VT and PETs, IoT and edge weight PETs with VT and PM at device and edge, web measurement weights TC with VT, AI and ML weight PETs with PM. IRM, LM and Data Minimization and Purpose Limitation (DMPL) are less often primary foci, signaling priorities for future work. The results provide a practical map and a replication-ready scaffold for assessment and updates.

</details>


### [4] [SemChunk-C: Semantic Segmentation for C Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23697)
*Boris Nazarov,Darya Frolova,Shaked Leibzirer,Pavel Kisilev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SemChunk-C，一系列轻量级语义代码分块模型（17M-150M参数），用于C族语言（.c, .cpp, .h, .cs等）的语义分割。该模型利用LLM分类器识别分块边界并为每个块分配功能属性（如数据结构、接口块等），在边界准确性和语义连贯性上匹配或超越基于更大代码LLM的分块器，并在下游任务中验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C族语言的语义分割因复杂语法、宏展开和不规则结构模式而具有挑战性。现有分块方法（固定窗口、启发式分割、语法工具）无法捕获有意义的函数单元，限制了检索和LLM下游任务的效能。

Method: 首先定义一组代码块类别；然后训练基于LLM的分类器，用于(a)识别块边界和(b)为每个块分配描述性功能属性（类别）；引入SemChunk-C系列轻量级模型（基于Ettin编码器，17M/32M/68M/150M参数），利用LLM的语义上下文捕获能力实现灵活的分块边界。

Result: 在多个数据集上达到高边界准确性和语义连贯性，匹配或优于基于更大代码LLM的分块器；在精心策划的基准测试中验证了改进的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 轻量级模型SemChunk-C能够有效处理C族代码的语义分块任务，包括嵌套定义和宏等复杂结构，为代码检索等下游任务提供更好的支持。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of code written in a C-family language remains a challenging problem, due to the language's complex syntax, macro expansion, and irregular structural patterns. Existing chunking methods, such as fixed-sized windows, heuristic splitting, and syntax-based tools, often fail to capture meaningful functional units, limiting the efficacy of retrieval and other downstream LLM driven tasks.
  In this paper, we address the problem of chunking in C-related languages. First, we define a set of code chunk categories. Second, we train an LLM-based classifier to a) identify chunk boundaries, and b) assign each chunk a descriptive functional attribute (a category), which can be useful for downstream tasks. By leveraging the LLM's ability to capture semantic context within the code, we assume flexible chunk boundaries, allowing to adapt to the specific structure and context of each instance. Third, we introduce SemChunk-C, a family of lightweight language models for semantic chunking of C-related files (.c, .cpp, .h, .cs, etc.). These models are based on the first four Ettin encoders [1] with 17M, 32M, 68M, and 150M parameters. Despite their relatively small size, they are capable of identifying cohesive code units, such as data structures, interface blocks, and other components. Furthermore, we demonstrate the robustness of our approach on real-world code, including challenging constructs such as nested definitions and macros.
  We test our approach on various datasets, and show that it achieves high boundary accuracy and semantic coherence, matching or outperforming chunkers that are based on much larger code-oriented LLMs. We also validate the improved performance of the downstream tasks on a few curated benchmarks.

</details>


### [5] [A Unified Framework for Runtime Verification and Model-Based Diagnosis in LOLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23720)
*Raik Hipler,Martin Leucker,Patrick Rodler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的框架，在流规范语言LOLA中集成了运行时验证和基于模型的诊断，通过将系统描述、组件健康状态和观测编码为单一的流式形式化体系，实现在线连续故障定位与检测，无需单独工具链，支持时不变、瞬态故障及非确定性观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时验证和基于模型的诊断通常依赖分离的工具链和形式化体系，导致故障检测与定位之间缺乏连续性，难以实现高效的在线故障定位。

Method: 在流规范语言LOLA框架下，将系统描述、组件健康状态和观测数据统一编码为流式形式化体系，利用该单一形式化体系同时支持运行时验证和基于模型的诊断，实现连续在线故障定位。

Result: 该方法支持时不变故障和瞬态故障，并能自然地处理非确定性观测，无需额外工具链即可在故障检测的同时完成故障定位。

Conclusion: 集成运行时验证与基于模型的诊断于单一流式框架中是可行的，能够有效提升在线故障定位的连续性和效率，适用于处理多种故障类型和非确定性观测场景。

Abstract: We present an integrated framework that unifies runtime verification and model-based diagnosis within the stream specification language LOLA. By encoding system descriptions, component health states, and observations into a single stream-based formalism, the approach enables continuous, online fault localization directly alongside fault detection, without requiring separate toolchains. The framework supports both time-invariant and transient faults, and naturally accommodates nondeterministic observations.

</details>


### [6] [ESAA-Conversational: An Event-Sourced Memory Layer for Continuity, Handoff, and Curation Across Heterogeneous LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23752)
*Elzo Brito dos Santos Filho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ESAA-Conversational框架，通过事件溯源技术实现异构LLM编码智能体之间的共享会话记忆，解决多智能体切换时的对话状态漂移问题。系统采用机械捕获（无需LLM推理）将对话转为不可变事件日志，并投影出决策、任务等读模型，通过PowerShell CLI提供完整工具链。570事件的自引用案例研究验证了异构智能体可通过共享日志协作，无需直接智能体间通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发者越来越多地使用多个LLM编码智能体（如Codex、Grok、Claude Code等），但由于各智能体将对话保存在私有、供应商特定的日志中，导致智能体切换时出现"对话状态漂移"——即一个智能体建立的目标、决策、待办任务和原理无法可靠地传递给后续接手的智能体。

Method: 1. 基于事件溯源架构（ESAA）的领域特化，将可见对话视为本地事件存储；2. 通过钩子（hooks）和监视器（watchers）机械捕获可见对话轮次，无需LLM推理；3. 将捕获内容归一化为追加式activity.jsonl文件；4. 确定性投影生成handoff.md、state.md、decisions.md、tasks.json等读模型；5. 通过领域命令（decide、task）支持用户显式策展持久性决策和任务；6. 提供PowerShell CLI工具链（init、enable-hooks、sync、project、verify、context、decide、task），支持workspace_root隔离和写路径锁文件。

Result: 1. 发布v1.1.0公开版本，作为绿色字段包（空公共日志）；2. 自引用案例研究使用570个开发实验室事件，证明异构智能体可通过共享日志协作，无需直接的智能体间通信通道；3. 公开分发版通过排除私有对话历史保护隐私。

Conclusion: ESAA-Conversational框架有效解决了多LLM编码智能体场景下的对话状态漂移问题，通过机械捕获和事件溯源机制实现了异构智能体间的无缝协作，同时保持了隐私安全性。

Abstract: Software developers increasingly work with multiple LLM coding agents, switching among tools such as Codex, Grok, Claude Code, and other assistants as context windows fill, sessions end, or a particular agent becomes better suited to a subtask. Each agent, however, persists its conversation in a private and vendor-specific log. The result is conversational state drift: goals, decisions, open tasks, and rationales established with one agent are not reliably available when another agent takes over. This paper presents \emph{ESAA-Conversational}, a domain specialization of Event-Sourcing Agent Architecture (ESAA)~\cite{esaa} for shared conversational memory across heterogeneous agents. The method treats the visible conversation as a local event store: hooks and watchers capture visible turns, normalize them into an append-only \texttt{activity.jsonl}, and deterministically project read models such as \texttt{handoff.md}, \texttt{state.md}, \texttt{decisions.md}, and \texttt{tasks.json}. Mechanical capture does not require LLM inference; agents use judgment only for explicit curation, recording durable decisions and conversational tasks through domain commands. The public v1.1.0 release implements a PowerShell CLI with \texttt{init}, \texttt{enable-hooks}, \texttt{sync}, \texttt{project}, \texttt{verify}, \texttt{context}, \texttt{decide}, and \texttt{task}; includes \texttt{workspace\_root} isolation and a write-path lockfile; and is distributed as a greenfield package with an empty public log. A self-referential case study with 570 development-lab events shows that heterogeneous agents can collaborate through a shared log without a direct agent-to-agent channel, while the public distribution preserves privacy by excluding the private conversational history.

</details>


### [7] [From Task-Guided Conversational Graphs to Goal-Oriented Dialogue Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23797)
*Mariano Garralda-Barrio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出目标导向对话运行时（GODR），一种面向高复杂度、多域、可中断的大语言模型对话场景的设计模式，将目标、任务框架、生命周期状态、失效规则和恢复契约作为一等运行时对象，以解决对话连续性无法通过代理身份、聊天历史或执行图位置可靠恢复的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图编排和多智能体框架虽使LLM工作流实用化，但在用户维护多个相互依赖的目标时无法保证对话连续性；目标可能被暂停、恢复、修订或因其他目标的动作而失效，当前方法无法可靠恢复这种客观连续性。

Method: GODR将目标（Goal）、任务框架（Task Frame）、生命周期状态（Lifecycle State）、失效规则（Invalidation Rules）和恢复契约（Resumption Contracts）作为一等运行时对象，将有限执行委托给图运行时、智能体、工具或API，形成框架中立的设计模式。

Result: 本文为概念性系统论文，未提供实证性能测量结果，而是将评估作为未来实证验证议程提出。

Conclusion: GODR并非替代简单引导流程中的工作流图，而是面向复杂、多域、可中断对话场景；论文形式化了问题、提出了运行时对象和架构选择标准，为后续实证研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Graph and multi-agent orchestration frameworks make production large language model (LLM) workflows practical, but they do not by themselves solve conversational continuity when users maintain several interdependent objectives. This conceptual systems paper focuses on the high-complexity end of that design space, where goals can be suspended, resumed, revised, and invalidated by actions in other goals. We introduce the Goal-Oriented Dialogue Runtime (GODR), a framework-neutral design pattern that treats goals, task frames, lifecycle state, invalidation rules, and resumption contracts as first-class runtime objects while delegating bounded execution to graph runtimes, agents, tools, or application programming interfaces (APIs). GODR is not proposed as a replacement for workflow graphs in simple guided processes; it is intended for complex, multi-domain, interruptible conversations where objective continuity cannot be recovered reliably from agent identity, chat history, or execution-graph position alone. The paper formalizes the problem, proposes runtime objects and architecture-selection criteria, and frames evaluation as an agenda for future empirical validation rather than as a measured performance claim.

</details>


### [8] [JupOtter: Cell-Level Bug Detection in Jupyter Notebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23877)
*Lukas Ottenhof,Thibaud Lutellier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JupOtter，一个专为Jupyter Notebook设计的缺陷检测系统，包含Notebook特定的分词策略、单元格级缺陷预测技术以及包含21,000+标注Notebook的数据集OtterDataset，在单元格级缺陷检测F1分数上超越静态分析器和大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jupyter Notebook从原型设计和交互式探索发展为用于复杂程序开发的工具，导致GitHub上存在大量有缺陷的Notebook，亟需针对Notebook特性的缺陷检测方法。

Method: (1) 保留单元格结构的Notebook特定分词策略；(2) 单元格级缺陷预测技术；(3) 构建包含21,000+标注Notebook的OtterDataset数据集。

Result: 在三个评估数据集中的两个上，单元格级缺陷检测F1分数超越了静态分析器和大型语言模型。

Conclusion: JupOtter有效提升了Jupyter Notebook的缺陷检测性能，证明了针对Notebook结构特性进行专门设计的必要性。

Abstract: Jupyter Notebooks are an increasingly popular coding environment used across many domains, especially in Python-based data science and scientific computing. Originally used for prototyping and interactive exploration, notebooks are increasingly used to develop more complex programs, leading to a rapid rise in buggy notebooks on platforms like GitHub. To address this trend, we present JupOtter, a bug detection system designed specifically for Jupyter Notebooks. JupOtter features three novel contributions: (1) a notebook-specific tokenization strategy that preserves cell structure, (2) a cell-level bug prediction technique, and (3) a new labeled dataset, OtterDataset, containing over 21,000 notebooks annotated for fine-grained cell-level bug detection. JupOtter achieves cell-level bug detection F1 scores that surpass static analyzers and large language models in two out of three evaluation datasets.

</details>


### [9] [Domain-Driven Design in Practice: A Mining Study of Maintenance and Evolution in Open-Source Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23984)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Yuhong Fu,Haowei Cheng,Mario Herb,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub上开源项目中的DDD战术构建块（Entity、Value Object、Aggregate、Repository等）进行了预注册的大规模实证研究，涵盖其静态分布、演化规律、限界上下文边界违规及其对软件维护质量的影响，填补了DDD领域缺乏大规模经验证据的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DDD在软件工程中具有重要影响力，但缺乏关于其实际实践中战术构建块的分布、演化及与软件维护质量关系的大规模经验证据，最新系统文献综述也呼吁进行此类实证评估。

Method: 通过GitHub Search API收集DDD相关仓库，采用自动化关键词过滤与人工相关性评估相结合的方法，围绕四个研究问题分析数据集：(RQ1)静态分布与共用法则，(RQ2)纵向演化，(RQ3)限界上下文边界违规的程度与维护影响，(RQ4)维护活动与构建块变更/边界违规之间的时间关联。

Result: 该研究将揭示DDD战术构建块在开源项目中的分布特征、共用法则、演化趋势，量化限界上下文边界违规这一DDD首要技术挑战的规模，并阐明维护活动与构建块变更及边界违规之间的时序关系。

Conclusion: 本研究将建立DDD项目维护与演化全生命周期的经验基础，为未来DDD工具支持和方法论改进提供实证依据。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design (DDD) is an influential software development methodology that structures software around business domain complexity through tactical building blocks such as Entities, Value Objects, Aggregates, and Repositories. Despite its prominence in software engineering, large-scale empirical evidence on how DDD is practiced, how it evolves, and how it relates to software maintenance quality in open-source projects remains scarce. This study presents a pre-registered empirical investigation of the distribution, evolution, and maintenance implications of DDD tactical building blocks in open-source GitHub repositories, addressing the call for large-scale empirical evaluations identified in a recent systematic literature review. We will collect DDD-related repositories from GitHub using the Search API, apply automated keyword filtering and manual relevance assessment, and analyze the resulting dataset through four research questions covering: (RQ1) the static distribution and co-usage of DDD building blocks across repository types, (RQ2) their longitudinal evolution over commit history, (RQ3) the extent and maintenance implications of Bounded Context boundary violations (the primary technical challenge in DDD adoption, unquantified at scale), and (RQ4) the temporal association between maintenance activities and building block churn or Bounded Context violations in DDD repositories. Together, these RQs trace the full maintenance and evolution lifecycle of open-source DDD projects and establish an empirical foundation for future DDD tool support and methodology refinement.

</details>


### [10] [Collaborative and AI-Supported Requirements Elicitation: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24060)
*Manoel Salgado Neto,Alan Araujo,Ronnie de Souza Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法控制实验，比较了四种需求获取方法（无AI支持的协作、Strateegia平台+GPT Writer支持的协作、直接LLM生成、基于讨论转录的LLM生成），发现结合协作与AI支持的方法产出的需求制品质量最高，且被认为比传统协作获取更清晰、更易执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM为需求获取提供了新的可能性，但缺乏关于AI支持的协作环境如何影响需求获取结果的实证证据。

Method: 采用混合方法控制实验，比较四种需求获取方法，使用ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148质量标准评估制品质量，并收集参与者对获取过程的主观感知。

Result: 结合利益相关者协作与AI支持的方法产出了质量最高的需求制品，且参与者认为该方法比传统协作获取更清晰、更易执行。

Conclusion: 生成式AI能够在保留利益相关者参与价值的前提下，支持将协作产生的知识转化为结构化需求文档，对AI支持的需求获取和人机协作需求工程具有启示意义。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation requires stakeholders to communicate needs, negotiate priorities, and collaboratively construct knowledge that can be transformed into software requirements artifacts. Recent advances in LLMs have created opportunities to support these activities through AI-assisted collaboration and automated artifact generation. However, limited empirical evidence is available regarding how AI-supported collaborative environments influence requirements elicitation outcomes. In this study, we conducted a mixed-method controlled experiment comparing four requirements elicitation approaches: collaborative elicitation without AI support, collaborative elicitation supported by the Strateegia platform and its GPT-powered Writer applet, direct requirements generation using a Large Language Model, and requirements generation from collaborative discussion transcripts using a Large Language Model. We evaluated the resulting artifacts using quality criteria derived from ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 and collected participant perceptions regarding the elicitation process. Our findings indicate that approaches combining stakeholder collaboration and AI-supported synthesis produced the highest-rated requirements artifacts and were perceived as clearer and easier to execute than traditional collaborative elicitation. The results suggest that generative AI can support the transformation of collaboratively generated knowledge into structured requirements documentation while preserving the value of stakeholder participation. We discuss implications for AI-supported requirements elicitation and human-AI collaboration in Requirements Engineering.

</details>


### [11] [Agon: An Autonomous Large-Scale Omnidisciplinary Research System Built on Prompt Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24177)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Jiaxuan Guo,Kejia Zhang,Jianda Du,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agon研究编排器，基于六大设计原则，通过444次迭代实验验证了“机器规模执行、人类方向引导”的新研究范式，并建立了可检查与需人工判断的失败分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使研究产出可规模化，瓶颈从生成制品转向判断主张。现有工作缺乏系统性地将可自动化验证与需人类判断相分离的研究编排框架。

Method: 构建Agon编排器，基于提示经济性、面向未来、最小提示、全学科、大规模并行、零代码六大原则。通过444次跨领域迭代循环验证，仅使用小型起始主题且无人工编写实验代码。

Result: 成功实现跨领域规模化部署，同时暴露新类型失败。建立按严重性、可修复性、可见性和能力定位四维度的失败分类体系，区分循环可自行发现修复的失败与需人类判断的失败。

Conclusion: Agon推动研究进入新范式：机器负责规模化执行，人类负责方向性引导。证明可检查部分可在工作流中自动验证，剩余判断留给人类科学家。

Abstract: Large language models are making research production scalable, shifting the bottleneck from producing artifacts to judging claims. We present \textsc{Agon}, a research orchestrator that validates what can be checked inside the workflow and leaves the remaining judgments to human scientists. \textsc{Agon} is built on six design principles: Prompt Economy, Future-Facing, Minimal Prompts, OmniDisciplinary, Massive Parallelism, and Zero-Code. We ran \textsc{Agon} across domains for 444 iterations of Prompt Economy loops, using only small starting topics and no human-written experimental code. These deployments demonstrate scalability while exposing new classes of failure. We organize these failures into a taxonomy along severity, fixability, visibility, and capability locus. The taxonomy separates failures the loops can see and fix from those that require human judgment. Together, these results show that \textsc{Agon} is pushing research toward a new paradigm: machine scales, human steers.

</details>


### [12] [AutoSpec: Safety Rule Evolution for LLM Agents via Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24245)
*Pingchuan Ma,Zhaoyu Wang,Zimo Ji,Yuguang Zhou,Zhantong Xue,Zongjie Li,Shuai Wang,Xiaoqin Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoSpec是一个通过反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）和归纳逻辑编程（ILP）自动演化安全规则的系统，在代码执行和具身智能体领域将规则F1提升至0.98和0.93，误报率降低达94%，且规则可解释、可审计、可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体自动化复杂任务时存在安全风险，现有方法面临两难：手工规则可解释但脆弱（高误报或高漏报），神经分类器缺乏可解释性，无法用于安全关键部署。

Method: 采用反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）结合归纳逻辑编程（ILP）的迭代框架：从专家规则和标注轨迹出发，迭代评估规则、挖掘假阳性和假阴性反例、利用ILP识别判别性谓词、生成候选规则编辑并验证，ILP高效剪枝指数级搜索空间。

Result: 在291条执行轨迹（代码执行和具身智能体）上，规则F1分别达到0.98和0.93，误报率降低94%，4-5次迭代收敛，ILP引导比启发式CEGIS的F1高4.8倍。

Conclusion: AutoSpec能自动演化出高精度、高召回、可解释的安全规则，且泛化到未见场景，解决了安全规则演化中可解释性与性能之间的权衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate complex tasks by integrating language models with external tools and environments. However, their autonomy poses significant safety risks: agents may execute destructive commands, leak sensitive data, or violate domain constraints. Existing safety approaches face a fundamental tradeoff: hand-crafted rules are interpretable but brittle, with overly conservative rules blocking safe operations (high false positives) while permissive rules miss unsafe behaviors (high false negatives). Neural classifiers lack the interpretability required for safety-critical deployments.
  We present AutoSpec, a framework that automatically evolves deployed expert-designed safety rules from user safe/unsafe annotations through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS) guided by inductive logic programming (ILP). Starting from the expert rules and a stream of annotated traces, AutoSpec iteratively evaluates rules, mines false-positive and false-negative counterexamples, uses ILP to learn which predicates discriminate them, generates candidate rule edits, and verifies candidates to select the best revision. The key insight is that ILP efficiently identifies predicates that appear frequently in false negatives but rarely in false positives (or vice versa), dramatically pruning the exponential search space of rule edits. This continues until convergence, producing interpretable rules that balance precision and recall.
  We evaluate AutoSpec on 291 execution traces spanning code execution and embodied agent domains. AutoSpec raises rule F1 to 0.98 and 0.93 across the two domains, achieving up to 94% false positive reduction while maintaining high recall, and converges within 4-5 iterations. The ILP-guided approach achieves up to 4.8x higher F1 than heuristic CEGIS. The learned rules are human-readable, auditable, and generalize to unseen scenarios.

</details>


### [13] [Architecting Hybrid Quantum-Classical Software Systems: Exploration of the Design Trade-off Space with Quantitative Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24260)
*Álvaro M. Aparicio-Morales,Jose Garcia-Alonso,Javier Cámara,Juan M. Murillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个混合量子-经典软件系统的架构风格形式化方法，能够基于用户QoS标准动态选择最合适的混合或经典配置，并成功识别决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算虽能解决特定问题，但覆盖功能有限且需要昂贵的专用硬件。混合（量子-经典）软件系统虽可通过SOA原则整合两种计算范式，但其设计和部署面临NISQ计算机的特有约束（如算法只能运行在特定机器、质量属性指标不一致）以及服务应用的结构和行为属性管理挑战。从SOA视角看，需要在不确定性下进行权衡分析并提供定量保证。

Method: 通过形式化混合应用的架构风格，提供一种探索量子-经典应用设计空间的方法，支持在不确定性条件下进行架构决策的权衡分析。

Result: 该方法成功识别了决策边界，能够基于用户的QoS标准动态选择最合适的混合或经典配置。

Conclusion: 该方法为混合量子-经典系统的架构决策提供了定量保证，能够在不确定性下实现最优配置的动态选择。

Abstract: Addressing problems beyond classical computing limits is sparking an increasing interest in Quantum Computing. However, despite their adequacy to address specific problems, quantum algorithms cover a limited subset of the functionality required in real-world computing systems. Additionally, they require expensive specialized hardware. To overcome this issue, hybrid (quantum-classical) software systems are emerging as a promising way to integrate both computing paradigms by applying the principles of Service-Oriented Architectures (SOA). Still, the design and deployment of hybrid service-based systems faces unique challenges like the idiosyncrasies and constraints of NISQ computers (e.g., algorithms that can only run in specific machines, disparate quality attribute metrics), and the management of structural and behavioural properties of service-based applications. From the SOA perspective, architectural decisions need to be made by performing a trade-off analysis and providing quantitative guarantees of system configurations under prescribed levels of uncertainty. In this paper, a method to explore the design space of quantum-classical applications is provided by a formalization of an architectural style of hybrid applications. The obtained results demonstrate that the proposed method successfully identifies decision boundaries. It enables the dynamic selection of the most suitable hybrid or classical configuration based on the user's QoS criteria.

</details>


### [14] [Detecting AI Coding Agents in Open Source: A Validated Multi-Method Census of 180 Million Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24429)
*Arsham Khosravani,Audris Mockus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多层检测框架，整合配置文件扫描、提交信息分析、作者身份匹配和机器人签名查找四种方法，对开源供应链中生成式AI编码代理的踪迹进行分类检测。发现单一方法仅能捕获部分活动：多方法检测识别850,157个Claude Code提交，而机器人账户查找仅恢复28,154个（3.3%），存在30倍相对召回差距。跨2024年12月至2026年4月的数据显示，代理每月产生超过32万次提交，Claude Code在17,295个项目中贡献886,122次提交。与独立PR普查对比，两种渠道捕获几乎不相交的代理群体，PR普查遗漏79%的提交检测到的Claude Code采纳者。工作特征取决于部署和检测模式而非工具本身，单一渠道不具有代表性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码代理正在进入开源供应链，但其多样且往往不可见的踪迹导致其普及程度难以理解，现有研究缺乏对代理活动的全面检测方法。

Method: 提出了一个多层检测框架，整合四种方法：配置文件扫描、提交信息分析、作者身份匹配、机器人签名查找。对World of Code中1.8亿+Git仓库进行分析，将代理踪迹分为四种行为类型。所有检测模式均经手工验证（495个标签），使用每个单元精度和Wilson置信区间评估。

Result: 单一方法仅能捕获部分活动，多方法检测识别850,157个Claude Code提交，而机器人账户查找仅恢复28,154个（3.3%），存在30倍相对召回差距。跨2024年12月至2026年4月，代理每月产生超过32万次提交；Claude Code在17,295个项目中贡献886,122次提交。与PR普查对比，两种渠道捕获几乎不相交的代理群体，PR普查遗漏79%的提交检测到的Claude Code采纳者。工作特征取决于部署和检测模式而非工具本身。

Conclusion: 单一信号普遍性估计值偏低至少30倍；单一渠道无法代表代理活动的全貌；不同检测方法捕获不同的代理群体和工作类型，需要多方法综合评估AI编码代理在开源生态中的影响。

Abstract: Generative AI coding agents are entering the open-source supply chain, yet their diverse and often invisible traces leave their prevalence poorly understood. We introduce a multi-layered detection framework that integrates configuration-file scanning, commit-message analysis, author-identity matching, and bot-signature lookup across World of Code (180M+ Git repositories), classifying agent traces into four behavioral types. No single method captures more than a fraction of activity: multi-method detection identifies 850,157 Claude Code commits in one snapshot, of which bot-account lookup_the signal most adoption studies rely on_recovers only 28,154 (3.3%), a 30x relative-recall gap, so single-signal prevalence estimates are biased low by at least this factor. Every detection pattern is hand-validated (495 labels) with per-cell precision and Wilson confidence intervals. Across snapshots from December 2024 to April 2026, commit-attributed agents generate over 320,000 commits per month; Claude Code leads (886,122 commits across 17,295 projects) and dominates silent, configuration-file-only adoption (21,078 projects). Compared against an independent pull-request census (AIDev), the two channels capture nearly disjoint agent populations_a PR census misses 79% of commit-detected Claude Code adopters and essentially all Codex adopters_and different kinds of work: PR-deployed cloud agents (Codex, Cursor) surface as feature work, while commit-deployed in-editor agents (Claude Code, OpenHands, Aider) surface as maintenance. The observed work profile follows deployment and detection mode rather than the tool itself, so no single channel is representative.

</details>


### [15] [Agentic Generation of AST Transformation Rules for Fixing Breaking Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24446)
*Frank Reyes,Benoit Baudry,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BigBag是一个基于智能体的框架，能够生成可跨项目复用的API级修复转换程序，在157个破坏性依赖更新基准上实现最高94.3%的可编译转换率和78.6%的修复率，跨项目修复率达33.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有修复方法在多个项目因同一库更新被破坏时，会为每个受影响的项目独立生成专用补丁，无法复用，导致修复效率低下。

Method: 提出BigBag智能体框架，生成结构化、可执行的修复转换程序，该程序编码API级别的修复逻辑，可迁移到任何受同一更新影响的客户端项目。采用四种大语言模型和两种AST转换引擎（Spoon和JavaParser）进行组合配置。

Result: 在BUMP基准的157个破坏性依赖更新上，最佳配置实现94.3%的可编译转换率和78.6%的修复率。跨项目修复率达33.3%，对于受影响客户端统一调用受影响API的情况，跨项目修复率达80%以上。

Conclusion: 智能体生成可复用修复转换程序是解决破坏性依赖更新的可扩展修复方法，证明了该方法的可行性。

Abstract: Modern software projects depend on third-party libraries that evolve continuously, introducing breaking API changes that prevent client code from compiling after a dependency update. When the same library update breaks multiple projects, existing repair approaches generate project-specific patches that cannot be reused, requiring each affected project to be repaired independently. We present BigBag, an agentic framework that generates fixing transformations: structured, executable programs that encode the repair logic at the API level and transfer to any client broken by the same update. We evaluate BigBag on 157 compilation failure breaking dependency updates from the BUMP benchmark, across eight configurations combining four large language models and two AST transformation engines (Spoon and JavaParser). The best configuration achieves a compilable transformation rate of 94.3% and a fix rate of 78.6%. Generated transformations transfer across projects, achieving a cross-project fix rate of 33.3% overall and 80% or above for breaking updates where all clients invoke the affected API element uniformly. These results show that agentic generation of reusable fixing transformations is a viable approach to scalable repair of breaking updates.

</details>


### [16] [Toward Self-Evolution-Ready Workflow Harnesses: A Reversible Migration Path and Convertibility Taxonomy for Expert LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24598)
*Yimo Lin,Zhen Zhang,Yibin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于Strangler Fig模式的逆向迁移路径，将专家验证的"LLM+脚本"静态工作流重构为可组合、可审计的模块化阶段，并通过三档可转换性分类（A/B/C）在系统路由层诊断工作流就绪状态并动态调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"LLM+脚本"工作流虽由专家验证并编码了宝贵的领域知识，但执行逻辑是静态的，无法根据反馈自适应调整；而现有智能体研究主要关注新构建的"绿地"智能体和合成基准测试，未解决遗留工作流的迁移问题。

Method: 提出一种可逆的Strangler Fig迁移路径，将遗留工作流重构为可组合、带类型约束、可审计的阶段；核心是设计了三档可转换性分类体系（A/B/C），将其作为系统编排层中的路由阶段，诊断工作流的迁移就绪状态并据此路由执行。

Result: 该框架实现了对遗留工作流的渐进式迁移，在不中断原有流程的前提下，支持从静态脚本到动态自适应执行的逐步转换。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了现有智能体研究忽视遗留工作流迁移的空白，为将静态领域知识工作流安全地演进为可反馈自适应的智能体系统提供了工程化的迁移路径。

Abstract: While expert-validated "LLM + script" workflows deliver significant value, they remain static: they encode hard-won domain knowledge yet fail to adapt execution based on feedback. Existing agent research predominantly targets greenfield agents and synthetic benchmarks, leaving the migration of active legacy workflows unresolved. To bridge this gap, we present a reversible, Strangler-Fig migration path that refactors legacy workflows into composable, typed, and auditable stages. Central to this framework is a three-tier convertibility taxonomy (A/B/C), implemented as a routing stage within the system harness, which diagnoses a workflow's readiness and routes it accordingly.

</details>


### [17] [Automated Summarization of Software Documents: An LLM-based Multi-Agent Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24689)
*Duc S. H. Nguyen,Minh T. Nguyen,Phuong T. Nguyen,Juri Di Rocco,Davide Di Ruscio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Metagente——一种基于LLM的教师-学生多智能体系统，用于生成软件文档的简洁准确摘要，实证评估显示其优于基线方法，并探讨了在软件工程中的变革潜力与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LLM和多智能体系统提升软件工程中文档摘要任务的相关性与精度，解决传统方法在需求分析和技术文档摘要方面的不足。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构，多个LLM智能体协作生成软件文档摘要。

Result: 在真实数据集上，Metagente在摘要任务中优于所有基线方法，有效提升了需求分析和技术文档的摘要质量。

Conclusion: LLM和MAS在软件工程文档摘要方面具有变革性潜力，同时指出了集成过程中面临的挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are revolutionizing software engineering (SE) by advancing automation, decision-making, and knowledge processing. Their recent application to SE tasks has already shown promising results. In this paper, we focus on summarization as a key application area. We present Metagente, an LLM-based MAS designed to generate concise and accurate summaries of software documentation. Metagente employs a Teacher-Student architecture where multiple LLM agents collaborate to enhance relevance and precision of produced summaries. An empirical evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates Metagente's effectiveness in streamlining workflows, outperforming the considered baselines. The evaluation provides evidence that Metagente improves summarization for requirements analysis and technical documentation. Our findings underscore the transformative potential of these technologies in SE, while identifying challenges and future research directions for their seamless integration.

</details>


### [18] [Bit-Precise Conformance Testing of Simulink Model Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24719)
*Daisuke Ishii,Takashi Tomita,Toshiaki Aoki,Hideaki Takai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文形式化了Simulink基本块类型的语义，并提出了一种测试方法，用于验证Simulink模型检查器（SLDV和SMT-based模型检查器）与仿真器之间以及不同检查器之间的位精确一致性。实验表明，SMT-based模型检查器通过了所有测试，而SLDV的通过率仅为80-96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Simulink缺乏覆盖所有模型元素及其数值行为细节的正式语义，现有的模型检查器（如SLDV和SMT-based模型检查器）的可靠性存疑，它们可能以与仿真器不同的方式分析模型。因此需要验证这些模型检查器的质量。

Method: 1)形式化Simulink基本块类型，包括定义块类型特征集和块的位精确行为；2)提出一种测试每种块类型在工具间位精确一致性关系的方法，通过测试套件通过率衡量：(i)模型检查结果与Simulink仿真结果的一致性，(ii)SmtMC与SLDV结果之间的一致性；3)在10种块类型上开展实验。

Result: SMT-based模型检查器高效通过了所有测试用例；SLDV在一致性(i)上的通过率为94-96%，在一致性(ii)上的通过率为80-90%。失败原因包括错误、边界情况和超时。

Conclusion: SMT-based模型检查器在Simulink模型验证中比SLDV更可靠，但SLDV在某些场景下存在明显质量问题，需要改进。

Abstract: MATLAB/Simulink provides a practical modeling language and a simulation engine for the development of cyber-physical systems. To ensure the quality of the developed models, there are formal verification tools available, such as Simulink Design Verifier (SLDV) and third-party SMT-based model checkers (SmtMC). However, due to the absence of a semantics of Simulink that covers every element of models and the details of its numerical behavior, the reliability of the model checkers themselves is often doubtful, potentially analyzing models differently from the simulator. This work aims to verify the quality of the Simulink model checkers by addressing the following items. 1) Formalization of the basic block types of Simulink. It involves defining block type feature sets and the bit-precise behavior of the blocks. 2) A method for testing bit-precise conformance relations among the tools for each block type. The pass rate of our test suites measures (i) conformance of model checking results with simulation results by Simulink and (ii) conformance between the results of SmtMC and SLDV. 3) Experiment to perform tests on 10 block types. We confirmed that SmtMC efficiently passed all test cases, while SLDV achieved pass rates of only 94-96% and 80-90% for conformance (i) and (ii), respectively. We analyzed the causes of failed tests, such as errors, corner cases, and timeouts.

</details>


### [19] [Two-Level vs. Multi-Level Modelling: An Empirical Study of Cascading Maintenance Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24721)
*Yuhong Fu,Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Georg Grossmann,Karamjit Kaur,Matt Selway,Markus Stumptner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于突变的多级建模（MLM）与两级建模（2LM）在协同演化中的实证比较框架，通过自动化一致性检查和预注册假设检验，验证MLM的结构统一能否减少变更后的不一致性和修改范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心定义变更会导致所有依赖制品需要更新，这是软件维护的核心级联问题。在MDE中，2LM范式将领域知识分散在元模型和模型制品中，需保持一致性，使协同演化成为不一致性和工作量的持续来源。MLM统一了这些制品，号称能减少协同演化负担，但缺乏与2LM的受控配对比较验证。

Method: 采用预注册的、基于突变的实证比较方法。从已发表的2LM协同演化场景语料库构建语义等价的MLM对应版本，对两者施加相同的演化突变，通过自动化一致性检查和预注册假设检验测量结果。采用阳性对照和盲法映射协议防止偏差。

Result: 提供了首个评估范式级结构选择是否影响级联维护负担的实证框架，将协同演化负担操作化为两个可自动测量的结果变量，并交付可复用的基准测试协议。

Conclusion: 该设计为验证MLM相对于2LM在减少协同演化不一致性和修改范围方面的优势提供了首个实证框架，具有可复用和可扩展性。

Abstract: When a core definition changes, every dependent artefact must be updated, a cascading problem central to software maintenance. In Model-Driven Engineering (MDE), the dominant two-level modelling (2LM) paradigm fragments domain knowledge across metamodel and model artefacts that must be kept mutually consistent, making co-evolution a persistent source of inconsistencies and effort. Multi-level modelling (MLM) unifies these artefacts and is claimed to reduce co-evolution burden, but this has not been tested in a controlled, paired comparison against 2LM. We hypothesise that MLM's structural unification yields fewer post-change inconsistencies and a smaller modification footprint than 2LM for semantically equivalent evolution scenarios. To test this, we present a pre-registered, mutation-based empirical comparison of co-evolution behaviour in both paradigms. From a curated corpus of published 2LM co-evolution scenarios, we construct semantically equivalent MLM counterparts, apply identical evolution mutations to both, and measure outcomes through automated consistency checking and pre-registered hypothesis tests. Positive controls and a blinded mapping protocol guard against bias. This design provides the first empirical framework for assessing whether paradigm-level structural choices affect cascading maintenance burden, operationalising co-evolution burden as two automatically measurable outcome variables and delivering a reusable benchmarking protocol for replication and extension.

</details>


### [20] [MANGO: Automated Multi-Agent Test Oracle Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24815)
*Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MANGO是一个多智能体框架，能够从自然语言描述中自动生成细粒度测试预言，用于VLA机器人系统的测试，在LIBERO_10和RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop基准上的实验表明其能有效检测并定位故障，提供更丰富的诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA机器人测试依赖人工构建的符号测试预言，成本高、需要领域专业知识、与特定任务和环境紧耦合、可扩展性差，且仅提供最终状态评估，无法定位中间行为故障。

Method: MANGO首先从自然语言描述中生成可复用的原子任务库，然后为每个原子任务生成基于模拟器的预言定义，最后通过将复杂指令分解为有序的原子动作序列及对应预言，生成可执行的细粒度预言。框架使用Generator、Assessor和Judge三个协作智能体，通过结构化反馈迭代优化生成产物。

Result: 在LIBERO_10和RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop基准上的评估表明，MANGO生成的细粒度预言能够检测与符号预言相当数量的故障，同时能准确定位故障位置并提供更丰富的诊断信息。消融实验验证了各组件贡献及初始任务集的影响，且保持了预言质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，面向VLA机器人测试的测试预言自动生成是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging robotic control systems that integrate perception, language understanding, and action generation in a unified architecture. Existing testing approaches for VLA-enabled robots rely on manually constructed symbolic test oracles that determine task success from final environment states. These oracles are costly to construct, require domain expertise, and are often tightly coupled to specific tasks and environments, limiting scalability and reuse. Furthermore, they provide only end-state assessments of task outcomes, offering limited insight into intermediate behavior and fault localization. To address these limitations, we introduce MANGO, a multi-agent framework that automatically generates fine-grained oracles from natural-language descriptions of robotic tasks. MANGO first generates a reusable library of atomic tasks, then generates simulator-grounded oracle definitions for each atomic task, and finally produces executable fine-grained oracles by decomposing complex instructions into ordered sequences of atomic actions and corresponding oracles. The framework uses collaborative Generator, Assessor, and Judge agents that iteratively refine generated artifacts through structured feedback. We evaluate MANGO on the LIBERO_10 and RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop benchmarks. Results show that MANGO generates executable, fine-grained oracles that detect a similar number of failures as symbolic oracles while accurately localizing them and providing richer diagnostic information. Through ablation studies, we further analyzed component contributions and the effect of initial task set, while preserving oracle quality. Overall, the results show the feasibility and effectiveness of test oracle generation for VLA-enabled robots testing.

</details>


### [21] [EnerInfer: Energy-Aware On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23001)
*Bohua Zou,Nian Liu,Binqi Sun,Matteo Mascherin,Debayan Roy,Yutao Liu,Yu Peng,Ning Jia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EnerInfer是一个面向端侧LLM推理的框架，通过利用配置松弛（适度降低NPU/内存频率）在保证服务质量的前提下实现能效优化与热管理，采用模型结构感知的预测与排名驱动的在线反馈机制，在真实设备上实现了最高65%的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端侧LLM推理系统主要优化解码速度，默认更快的执行总是更好的，但忽视了能效和热成本这一关键瓶颈。实际中设备存在可开发的配置松弛空间，适度降低NPU和内存频率可在保证用户体验的同时显著提升能效并减少发热。

Method: EnerInfer包含三个核心组件：1) 解耦的模型结构感知预测器，无需逐模型预配置即可预测不同NPU/DDR频率下的吞吐和功耗；2) 排名驱动的在线反馈机制，在运行时干扰下选择满足QoE的高效配置；3) 轻量级有限时域热预测模块，动态切换能效优化模式与热约束模式。

Result: 在实际LLM上评估，EnerInfer在手机上能效提升高达65%，笔记本上12%，开发板上24%，且均未违反QoE要求。

Conclusion: 端侧LLM推理存在可被系统化利用的配置松弛空间，通过模型结构感知预测与在线反馈结合的方法，可以在无传感器硬件支持下实现显著的能效优化和热管理，为端侧AI部署提供了切实可行的新路径。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is increasingly attractive for privacy-preserving, reliable, and cost-effective deployment, yet its energy and thermal costs remain a critical bottleneck. Existing systems primarily optimize for decoding speed, implicitly assuming that faster execution is always preferable. We show instead that on-device LLM inference often has exploitable configuration slack: modestly lowering NPU and memory frequencies preserves quality of experience (QoE) while substantially improving energy efficiency and reducing heat.
  Realizing this opportunity in production is challenging. The most energy-efficient NPU/DDR setting varies with the model, inference engine, platform, and runtime conditions, with no stable ranking across configurations. Commercial devices further lack component-level power sensing, and shell temperature evolves with request arrivals, response lengths, and thermal history. To address these challenges, we propose EnerInfer, the first on-device LLM inference framework that jointly manages energy efficiency, throughput, and thermal comfort for LLM workloads. EnerInfer replaces per-model profiling and sensor-heavy control with disaggregated, model-structure-aware prediction and ranking-driven online feedback. It predicts throughput and power for unseen LLMs across NPU/DDR frequency settings, selects QoE-satisfying efficient configurations under runtime interference, and uses lightweight limited-horizon thermal prediction to dynamically switch between energy-optimized and thermally constrained inference. Evaluations on real-world LLMs show that EnerInfer improves energy efficiency by up to 65%, 12%, and 24% on phones, a laptop, and a development board, respectively, without QoE violation.

</details>

### [22] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)
*Yashkumar R Lukhi,Harsh Rameshbhai Moradiya,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化的异构4专家混合专家（MoE4）架构大规模搜索流水线，在LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中运行。该流水线通过确定性代码生成器将LEMUR数据库中的基础架构家族组合成MoE4集成模型，并在NVIDIA RTX 4090上进行了28天的大规模搜索，生成了4,463个候选模型。研究发现由于使用itertools.combinations按字母顺序枚举，整个搜索空间（仅覆盖理论4.8%）被锚定在单一架构家族AirNet上。在此偏置范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3组合产生最高准确率集成（平均准确率0.632），而FractalNet和MNASNet被识别为低效家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化大规模搜索异构MoE4架构，克服手动设计的局限性，并在LEMUR数据集生态系统中系统性地探索基础架构家族组合。

Method: 采用确定性代码生成器自动组合LEMUR数据库中的基础架构家族形成MoE4集成，使用卷积门控网络、温度缩放、混合增强和余弦退火学习率调度；在RTX 4090上进行28天的大规模搜索。

Result: 生成了4,463个候选模型，其中1,021个成功评估；发现搜索空间（覆盖理论4.8%）因itertools.combinations枚举方式被锚定在AirNet家族；ShuffleNet+MobileNetV3组合达到最高平均准确率0.632。

Conclusion: 识别了搜索覆盖偏差及其根源，提出分层随机抽样修复方案；建议排除FractalNet和MNASNet等低效家族；流水线和分析工具已开源。

Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

</details>


### [23] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种离线强化学习损失函数（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在从大模型向小模型蒸馏推理能力时的机制差异。研究发现：SFT、RFT、RIFT的权重更新几乎共线（余弦相似度≥0.97），性能相当；DFT方向偏离更大；Offline GRPO在保持SFT损失盆地内引入了正交分量；DPO则处于近乎正交的子空间，存在模式连接障碍，但取得了最高准确率（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%），不过其使用了10倍小的学习率，性能差距部分归因于超参数差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从下游准确率角度比较离线RL损失函数，忽略了对这些方法在机制层面（权重更新方向、子空间结构等）是否真正不同或趋同的分析。

Method: 在相同数学推理数据上，使用Qwen3-4B基座模型和仅注意力层的LoRA，训练SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO六种方法；通过余弦相似度、主角度子空间分析、线性模式连通性、CKA四种指标分析权重更新的差异。

Result: SFT/RFT/RIFT高度相似（余弦≥0.97，GSM8K 87-88%）；DFT方向偏离更大；Offline GRPO引入正交分量但仍在SFT损失盆地；DPO近乎正交于其他方法，存在模式连接障碍，CKA在后期层降至~0.46，但GSM8K达93.5%，AIME26达30.0%，显著优于其他方法（3.3-10.0%）。

Conclusion: 不同离线RL损失函数在机制上差异显著，DPO学习到的表征与其他方法本质上不同；但DPO的优势部分来自其更小的学习率，需进一步进行学习率匹配的公平比较。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

</details>


### [24] [A Survey on Federated Causal Discovery and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23741)
*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果发现（FCD）与联邦因果推断（FCI）领域，通过多维度分类法组织现有工作，并建立了两者作为统一联邦因果推理流水线互补阶段的形式化联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理在数据驱动决策中至关重要，但可靠分析所需数据常分布在多个机构中，受隐私法规或通信限制无法集中。联邦学习虽可解决数据共享问题，但联邦因果发现与推断领域交叉性强且缺乏全面综述，阻碍了研究者进入。

Method: 基于FCD的三个核心设计决策（结构学习方式、数据划分方式、各方获得的结构知识），从方法论范式、联邦拓扑、结构范围三个轴组织综述；对于FCI，按目标估计量（平均vs个体化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（经典加权到现代深度生成架构）分类。最后形式化FCD与FCI作为统一联邦因果推理流水线的互补阶段。

Result: 提供了系统的多维度分类法框架，覆盖了时间动态、数据异质性、缺失数据、非相同变量集等关键实际维度，并明确了FCD与FCI在隐私、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域的共享关注点。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战与未来研究方向，建立了FCD与FCI作为统一流水线的形式化连接，为联邦因果推理领域提供了全面的入门指南。

Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers. This paper bridges that gap by providing a systematic review through multi-dimensional taxonomies. Grounded in the three core design decisions underlying any FCD solution, namely how structures are learned, how data are partitioned, and what structural knowledge each party obtains, we organize FCD along three axes: methodological paradigm, federation topology, and structural scope. We further examine key practical dimensions, including temporal dynamics, data heterogeneity, missing data, and non-identical variable sets. For FCI, we categorize methods by target estimand (average versus individualized/conditional treatment effects) and by estimation strategy, from classical weighting methods to modern deep generative architectures. Unlike prior works that treat FCD and FCI separately, we formalize their connection as complementary stages of a unified federated causal reasoning pipeline, where FCD supplies the structural knowledge required for valid effect estimation in FCI. Finally, we highlight their shared concerns regarding privacy, communication efficiency, theoretical guarantees, and application domains, and conclude by identifying open challenges for future research.

</details>


### [25] [Low-power analogue neural networks with trainable nonlinear connections for continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23742)
*Ian T. Vidamour,Fernando Aguirre,Thomas J. Hayward,Matthew O. A. Ellis,Charles Swindells,Alexander McDonnell,Martin Trefzer,Finley Robins,Luca Manneschi,Susan Stepney,Tony Kenyon,Oliver J. Sutton,Jack C. Gartside,Ivan Y. Tyukin,Adnan Mehonic,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受KAN网络启发，在物理神经网络的连接上放置可训练的非线性函数（模拟带通滤波器），在平滑连续值目标（如机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪）上比MLP更高效，但在分类任务上无优势。约35000个连接可转移至硬件，CMOS实现功耗约30微瓦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理神经网络将非线性器件响应强制作为标量权重，限制了其表达能力。受KAN网络启发，希望将可训练的非线性函数直接放置在物理连接上，使每个连接成为可学习的计算单元。

Method: 在可编程模拟阵列上实现模拟带通滤波器作为连接上的非线性函数，构建物理KAN网络。通过训练网络在多个任务上测试性能，包括机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪和分类决策边界任务。

Result: 平滑连续值目标上以更少的节点和连接超越MLP；分类边界上无参数效率优势；约35000个连接可高保真转移到硬件；CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦；忆阻器仿真复现相同行为。

Conclusion: 可训练非线性放在连接上而非节点上是性能优势的关键，而非特定器件类型。该方法在低功耗模拟计算和连续值控制任务中有潜力，但分类任务上不具优势。

Abstract: Physical neural networks promise low-power machine learning by computing directly with analogue device physics, but most architectures force nonlinear device responses to act as scalar weights. Inspired by Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we place trainable nonlinear functions on the connections, making each physical connection a learnable computational element. Realising these functions as analogue band-pass filters on field-programmable analogue arrays, we find that the benefit is task-dependent and follows from the smoothness of the physical basis: the networks represent smooth, continuously valued targets, including robotic kinematics, continuous control, and photovoltaic maximum-power-point tracking, with far fewer nodes and connections than multilayer perceptrons, but offer no parameter-efficiency advantage on classification-like decision boundaries. Trained networks transfer to hardware across approximately 35,000 connections with quantified fidelity, and a dedicated CMOS implementation is projected to operate at approximately 30 microwatts. A memristive realisation reproduces the same behaviour in simulation, indicating that the advantage comes from placing trainable nonlinearity on connections, rather than from a particular device.

</details>

Motivation: 离散反应拓扑与连续动力学参数紧密耦合，导致从稀疏含噪化学时序数据中提取可解释控制方程仍然困难。

Method: 提出PC-MCMC-CIGP灰盒工作流，包含：(1) spike-and-slab拓扑采样，(2) 硬守恒与热力学筛选，(3) 化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型用于参数校准与实验设计。集成各组件而非孤立提出新MCMC或GP。

Conclusion: 该集成工作流通过物理约束和不确定性感知采集选择，有效解决了反应拓扑-动力学耦合难题，优于常规黑箱方法。

Abstract: Extracting interpretable governing equations from sparse, noisy chemical time-series data remains difficult because discrete reaction topology and continuous kinetic parameters are tightly coupled. We present PC-MCMC-CIGP, a reproducible gray-box workflow that combines spike-and-slab topology sampling, hard conservation and thermodynamic screening, and a Chemical-Informed Gaussian Process (CIGP) residual model for parameter calibration and experimental design. The methodological contribution is not a new MCMC or GP family in isolation; rather, it is the integration of these components into a physically constrained workflow with explicit uncertainty-aware acquisition choices. On the H2 + Br2 benchmark, the constrained sampler distinguishes elementary radical pathways from deceptive phenomenological fits in our experiments. On styrene epoxidation, the CIGP optimization loop improves final yield by 12.5% over the reported GP-BO baseline. A new 10-seed acquisition study shows that EI, GWU, PC-EI, uncertainty sampling, discrepancy hunting, and random search have different trade-offs: PC-EI substantially reduces low-yield BO suggestions, while EI-style criteria give the strongest final-yield performance.

</details>

*Xiran Wang,Jian Zhang,Lei Qi,Yang Gao,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MEDIC的双元元学习策略，通过联合领域-类别匹配的隐式梯度匹配，同时优化域间和类间任务划分，以解决开放集域泛化中正负样本不平衡导致的过拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集域泛化方法中，使用一对多分类器时，正样本少、负样本多导致决策边界偏向正类，使得模型在未见域中过度拒绝数据（包括已知类），需要一种能平衡域和类别边界的解决方案。

Method: 提出MEDIC（双元元学习与联合域类匹配），通过隐式梯度匹配同时处理域间和类间任务划分，寻找对域和类别均平衡的最优决策边界。

Result: 在开放集场景下优于先前方法，同时保持有竞争力的闭集泛化能力。

Conclusion: MEDIC有效解决了开放集域泛化中类别不平衡导致的过度拒绝问题，且在开放集和闭集场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Domain generalization learns from multiple source domains to generalize to unseen target domains. However, it often neglects the realistic case of label mismatch between source and target. Open set domain generalization is then proposed to recognize unseen classes in unseen domains. A simple approach trains one-vs-all classifiers to separate each class and detect outliers as unknown. Yet, the imbalance between few positive samples and many negative samples skews the decision boundary towards the positive ones, leading the model to over-reject out-of-distribution data, even from known classes in unseen domains. In this paper, we propose a novel meta-learning stategy called dualistic MEta-learning with joint DomaIn-Class matching (MEDIC), which considers implicit gradient matching towards inter-domain and inter-class task splits simultaneously to find optimal boundaries balanced for both domains and classes. Experimental results show that MEDIC not only outperforms prior methods in open set scenarios, but also maintains competitive close set generalization ability.

</details>


### [28] [One Ruler: A Same-Hands Re-Evaluation of Bivariate Causal Direction on Tuebingen, with a Parameter-Free Compression Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23767)
*Wietse Stienstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了Tuebingen因果对数据集上的方法，在统一协议（相同102对、强制决策、无调参）下发现：文献中的排名存在严重偏差，一个零参数的排序条件压缩基线（bz2）达到74.7%加权准确率，与最强方法相当；SLOPE的82.4%是基于已决策子集的选择性报告，RECI的77.5%源于数据拷贝错误；方法实际聚类在低至中70%区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中不同方法在Tuebingen因果对数据集上的准确率比较存在严重不一致性：每篇论文使用不同的协议（不同子集、权重、模型选择和决策率），导致不公平的比较。作者认为这是错误的比较方式，需要统一基准下的公平重新评估。

Method: 1) 统一协议：所有方法在完全相同的102对上运行，强制每个对做出决策，不允许调参；2) 引入最小基线——排序条件压缩（sorted-conditional compression）：将量化、排序、一阶差分后的数据输入现成压缩器bz2，零可调参数；3) 忠实复现RECI和SLOPE等方法；4) 使用McNemar检验等统计方法评估方法间差异的显著性。

Result: 1) 基线在统一协议下达到74.7%加权准确率（p=3.7e-7）；2) SLOPE的82.4%实际是基于已决策子集的选择性报告，统一协议下为77.2%；3) RECI真实准确率为70.7%（而非文献中误引的77.5%）；4) 所有方法在统一量尺下聚集在低至中70%区间；5) 压缩得分大小是模型无关的混淆标记（p=2.8e-68）；6) 预注册的证伪测试以有启发意义的方式失败。

Conclusion: 文献中报告的因果方向推断准确率系统性地被夸大了，主要inflating机制包括测试集模型选择和显著性门控弃权。一个零参数的简单压缩基线即可与最复杂的方法持平，表明当前方法可能更多捕捉数据中的统计规律而非因果机制。作者呼吁未来研究使用统一协议进行公平比较。

Abstract: Headline accuracies on the Tuebingen cause-effect pairs are routinely compared across papers even though each is measured under its authors' own protocol -- different pair subsets, weightings, model-selection, and decision rates. We argue this is the wrong comparison and run the right one: a same-hands re-evaluation in which every method is run by us on the identical 102 pairs, with one strict rule -- no tuning and a decision forced on every pair. As a clean reference point we introduce a deliberately minimal baseline: sorted-conditional compression, which feeds quantized, sorted, first-differenced data to an off-the-shelf compressor (bz2) and has zero fitted parameters. Under the common ruler the ranking differs sharply from the literature. Our baseline reaches 74.7% weighted accuracy (p = 3.7e-7); on the same 100 pairs that SLOPE is evaluated on it scores 76.0%, a 1.2-point gap below the authors' own forced-decision SLOPE (77.2%) that is well inside noise (McNemar p = 0.39). A faithful re-run of RECI lands at 70.7% -- inside the original authors' reported error bar, not the 77.5% often quoted (which we trace to a mis-copied cell). SLOPE's published 82.4% is a decided-subset figure: scoring the authors' own stored output only on the pairs its significance test chose to answer reproduces 81.7%. Under the common ruler the methods cluster in the low-to-mid 70s and the zero-parameter compressor ties the strongest of them. We document the mechanisms that inflate published figures (test-set model selection, significance-gated abstention) and contribute two further results: compression score magnitude is a model-free confounding flag (p = 2.8e-68), and a pre-registered falsification test fails in an instructive way that bounds the method's theoretical interpretation. Code, pre-registrations, and per-pair outputs are released.

</details>

*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi,Li Erran Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurfBind是一个基于分子表面的表位预测框架，通过Transformer架构、表面补丁建模、结合子感知交叉注意力和层次化粗到细预测，在SAbDab和DB5.5基准上达到最优性能，并展现出对未知抗体和构象状态的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表位预测方法依赖序列或主链结构，难以捕获不连续的、表面驱动的构象表位，亟需一种直接基于分子表面表示的学习框架来编码抗体-抗原识别中的几何和物理化学模式。

Method: SurfBind框架包含：(1) 基于Transformer的架构进行patch级别的表面建模；(2) 结合子感知的交叉注意力机制；(3) 层次化的粗到细预测范式，直接在分子表面表示上整合几何和物理化学线索。

Result: 在SAbDab和DB5.5等具有挑战性的表位识别基准上，SurfBind达到了最先进的性能，并且在面对未见过的抗体和不同构象状态时展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 交互感知的表面建模对于理解蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用的关键机制具有重要价值，SurfBind验证了基于分子表面的方法在表位预测中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Molecular surfaces encode the geometric and physicochemical patterns that determine antibody-antigen recognition, central to epitope prediction. However, existing methods rely on sequences or backbone structures and struggle to capture discontinuous, surface-driven epitopes. This study presents SurfBind, a surface-centric learning framework for epitope prediction that operates directly on molecular surface representations. SurfBind integrates geometric and physicochemical cues through a Transformer-based architecture with patch-level surface modeling, binder-aware cross-attention, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine prediction paradigm. Experiments on challenging epitope identification benchmarks, including SAbDab and DB5.5, demonstrate that SurfBind achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across unseen antibodies and conformational states, highlighting the value of interaction-aware surface modeling for understanding the crucial mechanisms of protein-protein interactions.

</details>


### [30] [Reconstructing GRACE Terrestrial Water Storage with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks: An Application to South America](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23833)
*Lukas Arzoumanidis,Lara Johannsen,Klara Middendorf,Annette Eicker,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多变量时间序列图神经网络（MTGNN）将GRACE卫星观测的陆地水储量异常（TWSA）重建至1940年，通过学习ERA5气象强迫与GRACE数据之间的关系，利用混合邻接矩阵编码空间依赖关系，实现了高精度的长期TWSA重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRACE/GRACE-FO卫星提供的TWS观测起始于2002年，记录时间过短，不足以支持许多气候尺度的分析，因此需要将TWSA记录向历史时期扩展。

Method: 采用MTGNN架构，以每日ERA5气象强迫（降水、蒸散发、径流）为输入，学习与月尺度GRACE TWS异常之间的关系。混合邻接矩阵结合测地邻近性和气候时间序列的滞后相关性，同时编码局部水文耦合和大尺度遥相关。

Result: 网格单元Pearson相关系数0.69，流域平均相关系数0.94，近乎零偏差；成功再现2015/16厄尔尼诺和2020/21拉尼娜事件的空间特征；与GTWS-MLrec、RM-REC、GRAiCE等基线方法相比，统计性能相当（流域尺度相关系数差距在0.025以内），但所需预测变量仅为基线的1/2至1/10。

Conclusion: 图神经网络在TWSA重建任务上具有竞争力与效率优势，所有模型在干旱地区均存在特征性弱点，代码已开源。

Abstract: Terrestrial water storage (TWS) integrates snow, soil moisture, surface water, and groundwater and is a key indicator of how climate variability and human activity reshape the global water cycle. The GRACE and GRACE-FO satellite missions provide the only direct, globally consistent observations of TWS change, but their record only begins in 2002 which is too short for many climate-scale analyses. We present a deep learning application that reconstructs monthly GRACE-like TWS anomalies (TWSA) back to 1940 by learning the relationship between daily ERA5 meteorological forcing (precipitation, evapotranspiration, runoff) and monthly GRACE observations. In contrast to prior reconstruction approaches based on grid-cell-wise regression, CNNs, or LSTMs, we adapt a multi-variate time series graph neural network (MTGNN) architecture, which was originally developed for mobility and traffic forecasting on urban sensor networks to this satellite-geodesy task. Spatial dependencies are encoded in a static, interpretable hybrid adjacency matrix that combines geodesic proximity with lagged correlations of climatic time series, capturing both local hydrological coupling and large-scale teleconnections. The reconstruction achieves a grid-cell Pearson correlation of 0.69, a basin-mean correlation of 0.94, and a near-zero bias, and it reproduces the spatial fingerprints of the 2015/16 El Niño and 2020/21 La Niña events. A systematic comparison with established reconstruction approaches (GTWS-MLrec, RM-REC, GRAiCE) shows that the graph-based model is statistically competitive at basin scale, reaching a correlation within 0.025 of the best baseline while using only roughly half to a tenth of the predictors the other models require and revealing characteristic weaknesses in arid regions in all models. The complete implementation is publicly available at github.com/hcu-cml/MTGNN-TWS-Reconstruction-GRACE

</details>


### [31] [The Degeneracy Distillery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23838)
*T. Lucas Makinen,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"degeneracy distillery"的方法，通过估计和展平Fisher信息矩阵，自动且符号化地检测和解决物理模型中参数或标签的简并性问题。该方法从参数-数据对中探索似然的信息几何，发现能独立影响数据的符号坐标变换，从而在全局范围内展平Fisher信息，显著降低后续神经后验估计所需的模拟预算（最多可减少10倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数或标签产生相似数据时会出现简并性，这导致标签预测和逆问题求解困难。现有后验方法仅在单点展平，缺乏全局性；而识别简并性有助于理解模型选择或数据生成过程。作者旨在自动、符号化地检测和解析简并参数组合。

Method: 通过从参数-数据（或参数-模拟）对中估计Fisher信息矩阵，并对其进行展平处理。该方法探索似然的信息几何，将简并性表征为物理模型的内在属性，无需实际数据观测。通过发现符号坐标变换，识别对数据产生独立影响的参数组合，从而在全局期望上展平Fisher信息。

Result: 在合成和真实世界问题上的实验表明，该方法发现了符号坐标变换，在全局范围内展平了Fisher信息。相比仅在单点展平的后验方法，该方法显著降低了神经后验估计所需的模拟预算，在匹配验证校准的情况下，所需模拟次数最多减少了10倍。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动、符号化地检测和解决参数简并性问题，提供物理洞察，同时大幅提升下游神经后验估计的计算效率，减少模拟预算。

Abstract: When two or more parameters or labels produce similar data, they are degenerate, or hard to distinguish. Degeneracies render both label prediction and inverse problems difficult, since both machine learning algorithms and probabilistic samplers rely on the distinguishability of data and its gradients with respect to parameters. However, identifying degeneracies in physical models or real-world datasets can be elucidating about the choice of model or the underlying process that produces the data. We present the degeneracy distillery, a method that (1) detects and (2) resolves degenerate parameter combinations (a) automatically and (b) symbolically, from parameter-data (or parameter-simulation) pairs alone, through estimation and flattening of the Fisher information matrix. By exploring the information geometry of the likelihood, we characterize degeneracies as an intrinsic property of the physical model, requiring no realised data observation. We demonstrate our approach on a range of synthetic and real-world problems, discovering symbolic coordinate transformations that identify the combinations of parameters of a model which yield independent effects on the data. The resulting coordinates flatten the Fisher information in expectation globally, in contrast to posterior-based methods that flatten only at a single point, and substantially reduce the simulation budget required for downstream neural posterior estimation. In test cases we require up to $10\times$ fewer simulations for posterior estimation at matched validation calibration whilst simultaneously gaining physical insight on the system.

</details>


### [32] [Machine Learning Modeling for Real-Time Melt Pool Monitoring in Laser Powder Bed Fusion Additive Manufacturing: A Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23851)
*Inioluwa Emmanuel,Zhuo Yang,Ho Yeung,Xinyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI/ML在激光粉末床熔融增材制造中用于实时熔池监测的方法。采用1,200张镍基高温合金625熔池图像，对比了三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、MobileNetV2）与两种随机森林方法。最佳方法为EfficientNetB0特征嵌入结合随机森林（混合方法），在测试集上F1分数0.9451、准确率0.9458、AUC 0.9904，推理时间1.15 ms/张，表明预训练卷积特征结合经典集成方法可在数据有限的增材制造环境中实现高效稳健的实时熔池异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在激光粉末床熔融增材制造过程中，实时监测熔池状态对于保证打印质量和检测缺陷至关重要。然而，实际工厂环境中存在数据有限、计算资源受限、实时性要求高等挑战，亟需一种既准确又高效、且适合部署在开放式架构LPBF机器上的异常检测方法。

Method: 构建了二元图像分类框架，使用1,200张镍基高温合金625熔池图像（平衡数据集）。采用80/20训练-测试分割，训练集内再90/10划分验证集。对比五种方法：三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、MobileNetV2）和两种随机森林方法（基于EfficientNetB0特征嵌入的混合方法、基于原始像素特征的基础方法）。所有图像经标准化缩放、归一化及标签保持的数据增强处理。评估指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC，以及训练时间、推理延迟、CPU和GPU使用率。

Result: 混合EfficientNetB0+随机森林方法在测试集上表现最佳：F1分数0.9451，准确率0.9458，AUC 0.9904，单张推理时间1.15 ms（亚毫秒级）。纯深度学习模型推理时间显著更长且准确率更低。

Conclusion: 预训练卷积特征（EfficientNetB0）与经典集成方法（随机森林）的结合，为数据有限的增材制造环境提供了一条稳健且计算高效的实时熔池异常检测途径，兼顾了高检测性能与低推理延迟，适合工厂实时部署。

Abstract: This work investigates the implementation of artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) for real-time monitoring in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) additive manufacturing. We developed a binary image classification framework for distinguishing normal and abnormal melt pool images using a balanced dataset of 1,200 images collected from Nickel superalloy 625 on the NIST AMMT platform. The study evaluates accuracy and inference time based on control requirements and hardware limitations of open-architecture LPBF machines. We benchmark three transfer learning architectures (ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and MobileNetV2) against two Random Forest approaches: one trained on EfficientNetB0 feature embeddings (hybrid) and one trained on raw pixel features (baseline). Images are stratified into 80/20 train-test splits, with a further 90/10 validation split on the training set, and undergo standardized resizing, normalization, and label-preserving data augmentation to emulate realistic process variability. Each model is evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), along with training time, inference latency, and CPU & GPU usage to capture deployability constraints relevant to factory-floor monitoring. The hybrid EfficientNetB0-plus-Random Forest approach achieves the best performance on the held-out test set, with an F1 score of 0.9451, accuracy of 0.9458, and AUC of 0.9904, while maintaining sub-millisecond per-image inference (1.15 ms). In contrast, purely deep learning models exhibit significantly higher inference times with lower accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining pre-trained convolutional features with classical ensemble methods provides a robust, computationally efficient route to real-time melt pool anomaly detection in data-limited additive manufacturing environments.

</details>

*Konstantin Yatsenko,Arvind Thiagarajan*

TL;DR: Sesame是一个基于扩散模型的分子生成框架，通过创新的空间pairformer模块同时支持从头分子生成和基于片段的先导化合物优化，并在大规模配体-蛋白质复合物数据集上训练。

  <summary>Details</summary>
